# Status display inventory

Every status display in the Angular app as of AGT-3402 (2026-11-02), with the
tone it maps to on the shared [status indicator](status-indicator.md) and
whether it should move there. Follow-up AGT cards migrate from this table;
update the row (or delete it) in the same change that migrates a display.

Reading the columns:

- **Surface**: where the user sees it, `Area > Section`. Rows marked
  `(unmounted)` or `Unrendered` belong to components no template mounts
  today; they sort last.
- **File**: template path and the line of the first occurrence.
- **Tone mapping**: state to `success`, `warning`, `danger`, `info`,
  `neutral` or `running`. "proposed" marks a mapping for a display that has
  no tone in code today; "today" notes where the current colour disagrees.
- **Move to shared?**: `Done (AGT-3402)` is migrated; `Yes` moves to the
  default chip, `Yes (compact)` to the compact form; `No: not a status
  (tag|count|filter|...)` is not a status; other `No:` reasons keep their
  own form (lane tone under R9, banners and messages, interactive controls,
  dense chart marks, header and footer owned by AGT-3356).
